00002858          
00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
00002858                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
00002858                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
00002858                    ;
00002858          
00002858          
00002858          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)
00002858 E92D1FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12}                   ;r14 stored already
0000285C E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
00002860 E5CC0018           STRB    R0,[R12,#vuflags]
00002864          
00002864          
00002864 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
00002868 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
0000286C E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
00002870          
00002870          
00002870 E5992028           LDR     R2,[R9,#vuheights]              ;old heights for channels 1-4
00002874 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
00002878 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0
0000287C          .realvu_channelloop 
0000287C EBFFFFFE           BL      realvu_chanctrl                 ;returns R0=new average vu height for this channel
00002880 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002884 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00002888 E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
0000288C E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002890 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002894          
00002894          
00002894 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
00002898 E2866001           ADD     R6,R6,#1
0000289C E3360004           TEQ     R6,#4
000028A0 05893028           STREQ   R3,[R9,#vuheights]              ;store new heights for channels 1-4
000028A4 03A03000           MOVEQ   R3,#0
000028A8 0599202C           LDREQ   R2,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;old heights for channels 5-8
000028AC E156000B           CMP     R6,R11
000028B0 BAFFFFF1           BLT     realvu_channelloop
000028B4          
000028B4          
000028B4 E589302C           STR     R3,[R9,#vuheights58]            ;store new heights for channels 5-8
000028B8 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000028BC          
000028BC          
000028BC          .realvu_chanctrl ;r6=channel-1 (0-7)
000028BC E92D5FFE           STMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,R14}       ;
000028C0 E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000028C4 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000028C8 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000028CC C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000028D0 CAFFFFFE           BGT     realvu_blankchannel     ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000028D4          
000028D4          
000028D4 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000028D8 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000028DC DAFFFFFE           BLE     realvu_blankchannel     ;pitch is 0, so sample is blank
000028E0          
000028E0          
000028E0          .realvu_chanrunning

000028E4 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000028E8 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000028EC E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
000028F0          
000028F0          
000028F0 E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
000028F4 AAFFFFFE           BGE     realvu_blankchannel     ;channel is blank....
000028F8          
000028F8

000028FC B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002900 B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002904 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002908          
00002908          
00002908 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
0000290C B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002910          
00002910          
00002910                    OPT opt%
00002910 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002914 E1A00000           MOV R0,R0
00002918                    FNlong_adr("  ",10,loglintab)
00002918 E3A0B000           MOV     R11,#0
0000291C E3A0C080           MOV     R12,#128                ;get average real vu height from 128 bytes of DMA data
00002920          
00002920          
00002920          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002920 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002924 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
00002928 C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
0000292C C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002930 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002934 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
00002938 A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
0000293C A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002940          
00002940          
00002940 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002944 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002948          
00002948          
00002948                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
00002948          
00002948          
00002948 E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
0000294C                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
00002950 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
00002954 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
00002958 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
0000295C E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
00002960 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V
00002964 E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}
00002968          
00002968          
00002968          .realvu_blankchannel 
00002968 E3A00000           MOV     R0,#0
0000296C E8BD9FFE           LDMFD   R13!,{R1-R12,PC}        ;exit with R0=vu height for this channel
00002970          
00002970          
00002970                    ;
00002970                    ; SWI QTM_DMABuffer and fake DMA Buffer creation routine for 16bit sound RiscPCs
00002970                    ;
00002970          
00002970          
00002970          .swi_qtmdmabuffer 
00002970 E59C0078           LDR     R0,[R12,#fake8bitDMA]
00002974 E3300000           TEQ     R0,#0
00002978 059C00BC           LDREQ   R0,[R12,#DMABuffer]
0000297C 01A0F00E           MOVEQ   PC,R14
00002980          
00002980          
00002980 E92D4000           STMFD   R13!,{R14}
00002984 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope               ;update fake DMA block for 16bit-sound RiscPCs
00002988 E8BD8000           LDMFD   R13!,{PC}
0000298C          
0000298C          
0000298C          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
0000298C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
00002990 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
00002994 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
00002998          
00002998          
00002998 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
0000299C E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029A0 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029A4 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029A8 E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
000029AC          
000029AC          
000029AC E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
000029B0          .calcscope_channelloop 
000029B0 EBFFFFFE           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
000029B4          
000029B4          
000029B4 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000029B8 E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
000029BC E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
000029C0 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
000029C4 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8          .calcscope_chanctrl 
000029C8 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
000029CC E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000029D0 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000029D4 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000029D8 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000029DC CAFFFFFE           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000029E0          
000029E0          
000029E0 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000029E4 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000029E8 DAFFFFFE           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel
000029EC          
000029EC          
000029EC          .calcscope_chanrunning 
000029EC E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000029F0 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000029F4 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000029F8 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
000029FC          
000029FC          
000029FC E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002A00 AAFFFFFE           BGE     calcscope_blankchannel  ;go to clear the channel...
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002A08 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002A0C B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002A10 ABFFFFFE           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002A14          
00002A14          
00002A14 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002A18 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A1C          
00002A1C          
00002A1C          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A1C E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A20 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002A24 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002A28 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block
00002A2C E0911002           ADDS    R1,R1,R2                ;have we ended (offset becomes GE)?
00002A30 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002A34          
00002A34          
00002A34 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002A38 BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002A3C E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A40          
00002A40          
00002A40          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002A40 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002A44          .calcscope_clear ;
00002A44 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A48 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002A4C B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A50 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A54 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A58 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A5C B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A60 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002A64 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002A68 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit

00003664                    FNassm_mod      ;QTM_Mod  (incl. timer code and VU code)

00003664                    .CIAhandler                    ;r12 = song_data on entry
00003664 E92D4007           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}       ; * stack r0,r1,r14 and for v1.43se RasterMan, also store r2
00003668 E89C0003           LDMIA   R12,{R0-R1}            ;r0=CIA delay, r1=CIA counter
0000366C E59C2420           LDR     R2,[R12,#rm_CIAdec]    ; * v1.43se RasterMan
00003670                    
00003670          
00003670 E0511002           SUBS    R1,R1,R2               ; * take 1st lump (50Hz) of accurate time off CIA counter
00003674 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
00003678 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
0000367C DBFFFFFE           BLLE    ControlSong            ; * as we're now at 50Hz (but code expects 100Hz) so call twice!
00003680                    
00003680          
00003680 E0511002           SUBS    R1,R1,R2               ; * take 2nd lump (50Hz) of accurate time off CIA counter
00003684 D0811000           ADDLE   R1,R1,R0               ;if counter<=0 then add total CIA delay to it
00003688 E58C1004           STR     R1,[R12,#CIAcounter]   ;store CIA counter
0000368C E8BD4007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,R14}       ; * reload r0,r1,r14 and r2
00003690 C1A0F00E           MOVGT   PC,R14                 ;if CIA counter didn't run out, then exit
00003694                    OPT opt%

00003694          .ControlSong 
00003694 E92D4FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R11,R14}
00003698 E5DC0009           LDRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;load speed counter
0000369C E2500001           SUBS    R0,R0,#1                ;take off 1 (can be <0 if speed 0 used to stop song?)
000036A0 D5DC0008           LDRLEB  R0,[R12,#speed]         ;if counter<=0 then reload it with the speed
000036A4 E5CC0009           STRB    R0,[R12,#counter]       ;store counter
000036A8 CAFFFFFE           BGT     doFX                    ;if counter didn't get to 0, then go and do the effects...
000036AC          
000036AC          
000036AC E5DC000A           LDRB    R0,[R12,#patterndelay]  ;load pattern delay time
000036B0 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;is it 0?
000036B4 1AFFFFFE           BNE     waitdelay               ;if not, then go to the delay effects/count down routine

00003FA0 E8BD8007           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R2,PC}
00003FA4                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003FA4                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003FA4                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003FA4                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003FA4                    ;                                                             SH

0000426C          .do_tremolo ; ***** 07 - tremolo *****
0000426C E5DB000F           LDRB    R0,[R11,#currentvol]
00004270 E59B1028           LDR     R1,[R11,#tremolo_speed] ;0-3 depth, 4-7 speed, 8-9 wave, 10=no reset, 26-30 pos, 31 sign
00004274 E201331F           AND     R3,R1,#&1F<<26          ;=&7C<<24 ;get pos in bits 26-30 (bit 31 is sign, so clear it)
00004278 E3110C03           TST     R1,#%11<<8
0000427C                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R12,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &FF00
0000427C                    ;ADDEQ   R4,R4,#(vibratotable-song_data) AND &00FF
0000427C 024F4038           ADREQ   R4,vibratotable_new
00004280 07D43D23           LDREQB  R3,[R4,R3,LSR#26]       ;not ASR, as b31 just used to flag add/sub value
00004284 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     trem_set                ;PT prioritises 0/4 (sine)
00004288          
00004288          
00004288 E3110C01           TST R1,#%01<<8                  ;...then 1/5 (sawtooth)
0000428C 1AFFFFFE           BNE trem_rampdown
00004290 E3A030FF           MOV R3,#255 ;1 seems to work with 4mat test track  ;...then 2/6 (sqr, and 3/7 treated as 2/6)
00004294          .trem_set 
00004294 E201400F           AND     R4,R1,#&0F              ;depth
00004298 E0030394           MUL     R3,R4,R3                ;depth*value
0000429C E21124FF           ANDS    R2,R1,#&FF000000        ;R2=bits 26-30 pos (+b31 sign) (b24 & b25=0, because pos 26-30)
000042A0 50800323           ADDPL   R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042A4 40500323           SUBMIS  R0,R0,R3,LSR#6
000042A8 43A00000           MOVMI   R0,#0

000042B0 C3A00040           MOVGT   R0,#64
000042B4          
000042B4

000042B8          
000042B8

000042BC E0822B01           ADD     R2,R2,R1,LSL#(24-2) ;add speed to pos (bits 26-30 + b31 sign)
000042C0 E1A02C42           MOV     R2,R2,ASR#24     ;keep sign, move to bits 0-7 (v1.49 fix - vibrato was fixed in 1.46a!)
000042C4 E5CB202B           STRB    R2,[R11,#tremolo_pos]

00005414          .bytecompare 
00005414 E4D57001           LDRB    R7,[R5],#1
00005418 E3570000           CMP     R7,#0
0000541C 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     foundstring     ;if we've been through the whole sub string - we've found it...
00005420 E1540002           CMP     R4,R2           ;has the offset into the main string overflowed?
00005424 AAFFFFFE           BGE     notfound        ;if so, we can't find it...
00005428 E7D06004           LDRB    R6,[R0,R4]
0000542C E3560041           CMP     R6,#ASC"A"      ; }
00005430 A2866020           ADDGE   R6,R6,#32       ; }_ set letters A-Z **ON LY**           *** FAST
00005434 E356007A           CMP     R6,#ASC"Z"+32   ; }  to lower case (+32)                *** method!!!
00005438 C2466020           SUBGT   R6,R6,#32       ; }
0000543C E2844001           ADD     R4,R4,#1
00005440 E1560007           CMP     R6,R7           ;are the bytes from the main- and sub-strings the same?
00005444 0AFFFFF2           BEQ     bytecompare     ;if so, better check some more...
00005448 E2833001           ADD     R3,R3,#1
0000544C E1530002           CMP     R3,R2           ;run out of place to look?
00005450 BAFFFFED           BLT     searchloop      ;- no, so loop back!

00005518          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
00005618                    OPT opt%
00005618          
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
00005618                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
00005618                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
00005618                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
00005618                    ;
00005618          
00005618          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
00005618 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
0000561C E24F2008           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
00005620          
00005620                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
00005620 E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
00005624 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
00005628          .shuffleloop 
00005628 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
0000562C E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
00005630 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
00005634 0AFFFFFE           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
00005638          
00005638          
00005638 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
0000563C 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00005640          
00005640          
00005640 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
00005644 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
00005648 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
0000564C E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
00005650 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
00005654          .skipthisshuffle 
00005654 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
00005658 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
0000565C E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
00005660 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
00005664 EAFFFFEF           B       shuffleloop         ;keep going until end of table
00005668          
00005668

000056A8          
000056A8                    ; on entry
000056A8          
000056A8                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
000056A8                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
000056A8                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
000056A8                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
000056A8                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
000056A8                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
000056A8                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
000056A8                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
000056A8                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
000056A8                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
000056A8                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
000056A8                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
000056A8                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
000056A8                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
000056A8                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
000056A8

00006868                    ;R1=den

00002350          .tmr_setlinetime 
00002350                    ;
00002350                    ; R9  s=speed
00002350                    ; R10 c=CIA tempo
00002350                    ;
00002350                    ; equation; time per line=10s/4c (*65536 for accuracy)

00002368 E8BD8003           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R1,PC}

0000236C          
0000236C                    ; R12=song_data
0000236C                    ; R11=address of current word
0000236C                    ; R10=CIA tempo (32-255)
0000236C                    ; R9=speed (1-31)
0000236C                    ; R8=time per line = (10*speed/4*cia tempo)*65536
0000236C                    ; R7=time so far
0000236C                    ; R6=channel-1 (0-4/6/8)
0000236C                    ; R5=current word
0000236C                    ; R4=sequence
0000236C                    ; R3=event

00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; New real VU code 18/4/2001
00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; This code calculates the VU levels by examining the samples QTM is currently playing
00002858                    ; rather than scanning the RISC OS DMA buffer data and calculating the levels from there.
00002858                    ;
00002858                    ; The new routine produces identical output to scanning the DMA buffer (on 8bit sound comps)
00002858                    ; however now works on RiscPCs with 16bit sound, which do not have an 8bit sound buffer!
00002858                    ;
00002858          
00002858          
00002858          .realVUs  ;new realVUs code (gives correct result on 16bit sound)

0000286C E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
00002870          
00002870

00002874 E3A03000           MOV     R3,#0                           ;new heights for channels 1-4, 5-8
00002878 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0

00002880 E20210FF           AND     R1,R2,#&FF                      ;old average vu height for this channel
00002884 E0811000           ADD     R1,R1,R0
00002888 E18330A1           ORR     R3,R3,R1,LSR#1                  ;put averaged value in R3
0000288C E1A03463           MOV     R3,R3,ROR#8
00002890 E1A02462           MOV     R2,R2,ROR#8
00002894          
00002894

000028CC C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)

00002920          .realvu_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002920 E7D0E641           LDRB    R14,[R0,R1,ASR#12]      ;load a byte of sample data
00002924 E05EE003           SUBS    R14,R14,R3              ;scale for volume
00002928 C3CEE001           BICGT   R14,R14,#%00000001      ;make +ve log (=even no.s)
0000292C C7DAE00E           LDRGTB  R14,[R10,R14]           ;load +ve linear byte (0-127)
00002930 C08BB00E           ADDGT   R11,R11,R14             ;if>0 accumulate
00002934 E0911082           ADDS    R1,R1,R2,LSL#1          ;add pitch*2, have we ended? (offset becomes GE)?
00002938 A0511605           SUBGES  R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start (and check offset now ok)
0000293C A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if offset still -ve, get back to repeat start
00002940          
00002940          
00002940 E25CC002           SUBS    R12,R12,#2
00002944 CAFFFFF5           BGT     realvu_sampleloop       ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002948          
00002948          
00002948                    ; now R11 has 64*bytes (0 to 127), so need to divide by 128 (= >>7)to get value 0-63
00002948          
00002948          
00002948 E1A003AB           MOV     R0,R11,LSR#7            ;return R0=average vu level (0-64) for this DMA fill
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C                    ;ADD     R0,R0,R0,LSR#2          ;**old** linear scale-up so the vu fills 0-64 better
0000294C                    ;AND     R0,R0,#63               ; (..this on does 0-80, then clips down to 0-63!)
0000294C          
0000294C          
0000294C E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;better log sq'd scale-up routine (T's ;-)
00002950 E1A01000           MOV     R1,R0                   ;
00002954 E0000091           MUL     R0,R1,R0                ;
00002958 E1A00320           MOV     R0,R0,LSR#6             ;
0000295C E2600040           RSB     R0,R0,#64               ;this does 0-64 on a log (sq'd) curve, so low vols are more amp'd
00002960 E1500000           CMP     R0,R0 ; Clear V

0000298C          
0000298C          .calcscope ;called by SWI QTM_DMABuffer on 16bit sound RiscPCs
0000298C E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}               ;to generate a fake 8bit sound DMA buffer for progs use!
00002990 E1A0900C           MOV     R9,R12                          ;R9=start of QTM vars block (R12 usually)
00002994 E599C078           LDR     R12,[R9,#fake8bitDMA]           ;R12=fake DMA address
00002998          
00002998          
00002998 E2898D0B           ADD     R8,R9,#(DMA_blocks-song_data)   ;R8=channel DMA data
0000299C E5D9B092           LDRB    R11,[R9,#chans_use]             ;R11=4 or 8 chans (=4 or 8 bytes to skip each chan byte)
000029A0 E08CA20B           ADD     R10,R12,R11,LSL#4
000029A4 E08AA30B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#6               ;v1.45
000029A8 E08AA38B           ADD     R10,R10,R11,LSL#7               ;***** fake DMA is always 208 bytes * no. of channels
000029AC          
000029AC          
000029AC E1A0600B           MOV     R6,R11
000029B0          .calcscope_channelloop 
000029B0 EB000004           BL      calcscope_chanctrl
000029B4          
000029B4          
000029B4 E2888018           ADD     R8,R8,#24
000029B8 E28CC001           ADD     R12,R12,#1
000029BC E2566001           SUBS    R6,R6,#1
000029C0 CAFFFFFA           BGT     calcscope_channelloop
000029C4 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
000029C8          
000029C8          
000029C8          .calcscope_chanctrl 
000029C8 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}       ;
000029CC E898003F           LDMIA   R8,{R0-R5}              ;r0=end addr, r1=-ve offset, r2=pitch, r3=vol/flag, r4=rlen
000029D0 E3710A01           CMN     R1,#4096                ;is offset >-4096? (ie. offset>>12 <1)?   (remember -ve!)
000029D4 C3A07003           MOVGT   R7,#3                   ; <put min loop len in r7>
000029D8 C1570005           CMPGT   R7,R5                   ; <backwards!!! this does CMP 3,len so LE if valid!!!)
000029DC CA000017           BGT     calcscope_blankchannel  ;if offset>-4096 and 3>repeat len, then exit
000029E0          
000029E0          
000029E0 E3500000           CMP     R0,#0                   ;v1.45 - huge bug - can't believe address=0 worked for so long!
000029E4 13520000           CMPNE   R2,#0
000029E8 DA000014           BLE     calcscope_blankchannel
000029EC          
000029EC          
000029EC          .calcscope_chanrunning 
000029EC E1B03C03           MOVS    R3,R3,LSL#24            ;if DMA_sampleflag set, CS because &FF shifted off
000029F0 32897FF1           ADDCC   R7,R9,#(musicvoltable-song_data)
000029F4 22897D73           ADDCS   R7,R9,#(samplevoltable-song_data)
000029F8 E7D73C23           LDRB    R3,[R7,R3,LSR#24]       ;load Arc scaled volume
000029FC          
000029FC          
000029FC E35300FE           CMP     R3,#254                 ;if volume is too low
00002A00 AA00000E           BGE     calcscope_blankchannel  ;go to clear the channel...
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04          
00002A04 E3520B01           CMP     R2,#1024                        ;
00002A08 B2897C0B           ADDLT   R7,R9,#(arcperiods-song_data)
00002A0C B7972102           LDRLT   R2,[R7,R2,LSL#2]                ;load Arc pitch increment
00002A10 AB000E08           BLGE    amigatoarcpitch_1024            ;for high periods, use calculation - returns r2=arc pitch
00002A14          
00002A14          
00002A14 E3550002           CMP     R5,#2                   ;is this sample a looping sample?
00002A18 B3A05002           MOVLT   R5,#2                   ;if not, make repoff=2, so when sample ends, last 2 bytes repeat
00002A1C          
00002A1C          
00002A1C          .calcscope_sampleloop ;this code handles samples which play/repeat/end at any vol
00002A1C E7D06641           LDRB    R6,[R0,R1,ASR#12]       ;load a byte of sample data
00002A20 E0566003           SUBS    R6,R6,R3                ;scale for volume
00002A24 43A06000           MOVMI   R6,#0                   ;reset to 0 if vol too low
00002A28 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;store in fake DMA block
00002A2C E0911002           ADDS    R1,R1,R2                ;have we ended (offset becomes GE)?
00002A30 A0411605           SUBGE   R1,R1,R5,LSL#12         ;...if so, get back to repeat start
00002A34          
00002A34          
00002A34 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;check fake DMA end...
00002A38 BAFFFFF7           BLT     calcscope_sampleloop    ;if fake DMA ok and still playing, then loop back
00002A3C E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00002A40          
00002A40          
00002A40          .calcscope_blankchannel 
00002A40 E3A06000           MOV     R6,#0                   ;
00002A44          .calcscope_clear ;
00002A44 E6CC600B           STRB    R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A48 E15C000A           CMP     R12,R10                 ;
00002A4C B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A50 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A54 B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A58 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;
00002A5C B6CC600B           STRLTB  R6,[R12],R11            ;
00002A60 B15C000A           CMPLT   R12,R10                 ;only using 4 blankings per loop...
00002A64 BAFFFFF6           BLT     calcscope_clear         ;...cleared rest of channel
00002A68 E8BD9FFF           LDMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,PC}        ;exit
00002A6C                    FNvucode

00003FA4                    ; - this very rare effect inverts actual sample data (in memory),
00003FA4                    ;   and is the only effect NOT included in QTM<v1.48. Although I
00003FA4                    ;   coded the routine, I never found a song which uses it, until
00003FA4                    ;   4mat provided a test track ;) thanks! Now fully implememnted.
00003FA4                    ;                                                             SH
00003FA4          .funktable 
00003FA4 00                 EQUB   0
00003FA5 05                 EQUB   5
00003FA6 06                 EQUB   6
00003FA7 07                 EQUB   7
00003FA8 08                 EQUB   8
00003FA9 0A                 EQUB  10
00003FAA 0B                 EQUB  11
00003FAB 0D                 EQUB  13
00003FAC 10                 EQUB  16
00003FAD 13                 EQUB  19
00003FAE 16                 EQUB  22
00003FAF 1A                 EQUB  26
00003FB0 20                 EQUB  32
00003FB1 2B                 EQUB  43
00003FB2 40                 EQUB  64
00003FB3 80                 EQUB 128
00003FB4                    ALIGN

0000540C          .searchloop

00005410 E1A05001           MOV     R5,R1
00005414          .bytecompare 
00005414 E4D57001           LDRB    R7,[R5],#1
00005418 E3570000           CMP     R7,#0
0000541C 0A00000F           BEQ     foundstring     ;if we've been through the whole sub string - we've found it...
00005420 E1540002           CMP     R4,R2           ;has the offset into the main string overflowed?
00005424 AA00000A           BGE     notfound        ;if so, we can't find it...
00005428 E7D06004           LDRB    R6,[R0,R4]
0000542C E3560041           CMP     R6,#ASC"A"      ; }
00005430 A2866020           ADDGE   R6,R6,#32       ; }_ set letters A-Z **ON LY**           *** FAST
00005434 E356007A           CMP     R6,#ASC"Z"+32   ; }  to lower case (+32)                *** method!!!
00005438 C2466020           SUBGT   R6,R6,#32       ; }
0000543C E2844001           ADD     R4,R4,#1
00005440 E1560007           CMP     R6,R7           ;are the bytes from the main- and sub-strings the same?
00005444 0AFFFFF2           BEQ     bytecompare     ;if so, better check some more...
00005448 E2833001           ADD     R3,R3,#1
0000544C E1530002           CMP     R3,R2           ;run out of place to look?
00005450 BAFFFFED           BLT     searchloop      ;- no, so loop back!
00005454          .notfound 
00005454 E1B030A3           MOVS    R3,R3,LSR #1 ; Clear C
00005458 E8BD00F8           LDMFD   R13!,{R3-R7}

00005518          .loglintab ;this is precalculated and loaded from a data file at assembly
00005618                    OPT opt%
00005618          
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; Ultra compact pattern-shuffle, (c) STEP Harrison 2013
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; uses precalculated table (64 bytes) and 80 bytes of code = 144 bytes total
00005618                    ; in order to reshuffle 2048 bytes of pattern data using no additional memory
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; input pattern format c1-4 events 0,1,2...63 then c5-8 events 0,1,2...63
00005618                    ; output pattern format c1-8 events 0,1,2,3....61,62,63
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; shuffle example input  0,1,2,3,4,5.....124,125,126,127
00005618                    ;         example output 0,64,1,65,2,66,3,67,4....125,62,126,63,127
00005618                    ;
00005618                    ; shuffle table calculated by hand/using spreadsheet...is there a better way?
00005618                    ;
00005618          
00005618          .shufflepattern ;reorder StarTrekker patters into FastTracker format
00005618 E92D5FFF           STMFD   R13!,{R0-R12,R14}   ;R0=start addr of 8 channel pattern to reshuffe
0000561C E28F2044           ADR     R2,shuffletable     ;(alternate one offset from table, one from R1)
00005620          
00005620                    ;if table byte=0, skip, if table byte=255 end
00005620 E2801B01           ADD     R1,R0,#64*16        ;(64 to 127, use inbetween table bytes)
00005624 E1A03000           MOV     R3,R0               ;output address
00005628          .shuffleloop 
00005628 E4D24001           LDRB    R4,[R2],#1          ;load table byte
0000562C E3340000           TEQ     R4,#0               ;0=skip
00005630 02833010           ADDEQ   R3,R3,#1*16         ;get to next event position
00005634 0A000006           BEQ     skipthisshuffle
00005638          
00005638          
00005638 E33400FF           TEQ     R4,#255             ;255=exit
0000563C 08BD9FFF           LDMEQFD R13!,{R0-R12,PC}
00005640          
00005640          
00005640 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;load 4 channels from current event
00005644 E0804204           ADD     R4,R0,R4,LSL#4
00005648 E8941E00           LDMIA   R4,{R9,R10,R11,R12} ;load 4 channels from destination event
0000564C E88401E0           STMIA   R4,{R5,R6,R7,R8}
00005650 E8A31E00           STMIA   R3!,{R9,R10,R11,R12};swap them round and increment to next event
00005654          .skipthisshuffle 
00005654 E89301E0           LDMIA   R3,{R5,R6,R7,R8}    ;...do the next event using R1 as swap address
00005658 E8911E00           LDMIA   R1,{R9,R10,R11,R12}
0000565C E8A101E0           STMIA   R1!,{R5,R6,R7,R8}

000056A8                    ; on entry
000056A8          
000056A8                    ;          R0          \  sample end address            - sample end address
000056A8                    ;          R1           } -ve sample offset             - -ve sample offset
000056A8                    ;          R2           } (amiga type) pitch            - sample pitch increment (Arc)
000056A8                    ;          R3           } linear volume (0-64)          - sample volume (0-255)
000056A8                    ;          R4           } -ve repeat offset             - -ve repeat offset
000056A8                    ;          R5          /                                - load/store sample byte
000056A8                    ;          R6      -ve if channel handler changed       - channel counter (0->channel-1)
000056A8                    ;          R7      <unknown>                            -
000056A8                    ;          R8      uS <? - unknown>                     - ptr to 'DMA_blocks'
000056A8                    ;          R9      ptr to initblock                     - ptr to 'song_data' (usually R12)
000056A8                    ;          R10     DMA end                              - DMA end
000056A8                    ;          R11     DMA gap                              - DMA gap
000056A8                    ;          R12     DMA start                            - DMA ptr
000056A8                    ;          R13     stack                                - stack
000056A8                    ;          R14     exit                                 - end
000056A8

00006868                    ; Trial of improved and shorter looped DIV code
00006868          
00006868                    ; on entry - R0=A +ve; R1=B +ve >0
00006868                    ; on exit - R0=A DIV B; R1=A MOD B
00006868          
00006868                    ;R0=num
00006868                    ;R1=den
00006868                    ;R2=cnt
00006868                    ;R3=mod
00006868          
00006868                    ; seems to equal the unrolled AU div routine when dividing large 32 bit numbers by 512+
00006868
